### 2026-07-21 14:09:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Non Farm Payrolls #Importance 1

UK Payroll Employment Down 4K in June — The number of payrolled employees in the UK decreased by 4 thousand to 30.3 million in June 2026, following an upwardly revised increase of 3 thousand in May. Compared with a year earlier, employment fell by 0.2%, or 71 thousand, reflecting continued weakness in hiring. The accommodation and food service sector recorded the biggest annual decline in payroll employment, shedding 79,000 jobs, whereas the health and social work sector recorded the largest increase, adding 39,000 employees. Across NUTS3 regions, Westminster experienced a 2.0% drop in payrolled employees, while Mid Ulster recorded 2.1% growth. Meanwhile, median monthly pay rose by 4.3% year-on-year to £2,632, easing from a 4.6% increase in May. Growth in median pay was highest in the health and social work sector (+6.2%) and lowest in the education sector (+3.5%).

### 2026-07-21 14:04:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Closes 0.3% Lower After Hot Inflation Data — The NZX 50 fell 40 points, or 0.3%, to close at 13,656 on Tuesday, after being flat in the previous session, as traders assessed inflation data and evaluated its impact on the central bank's interest rate decision at the upcoming meeting. The latest data showed that New Zealand's inflation rate accelerated to the highest level since Q4 2023. Persistently high oil prices also pressured sentiment, as inflation concerns fueled expectations of further interest rate hikes. RBNZ Chief Economist Paul Conway recently warned that sticky inflation could prompt further monetary policy tightening. A decline on Wall Street overnight also further pressured sentiment amid escalating tensions in the Middle East. Communication services, consumer staples, and healthcare stocks mainly weighed on the index, with the biggest laggards including A2 Milk (-1.8%), Ebos Group (-1.7%), Freightways Group (-1.6%), Fisher & Paykel Healthcare (-1.5%), and Scales Corporation (-1.5%).

European Stocks Set for Subdued Open — European equity markets were set to open slightly lower on Tuesday as investors continued to monitor ongoing hostilities between the US and Iran, which entered a tenth consecutive day. However, mediators were working to bring both sides back to the negotiating table, while reports pointed to a possible 10-day ceasefire. In Europe, investors will focus on UK unemployment figures and economic sentiment data from Germany and the Eurozone, while looking ahead to an expected interest rate hold from the European Central Bank later this week. Meanwhile, there are no major corporate earnings releases scheduled in Europe on Tuesday. In premarket trading, Euro Stoxx 50 and Stoxx 600 futures were down 0.1% and 0.2%, respectively.

### 2026-07-21 13:45:27  #宏觀

【《山西省統籌煤炭行業發展和安全新規十七條》擬釋出：將常態化開展違法“四鏈”整治】《山西省統籌煤炭行業發展和安全新規十七條》即將釋出，現公開徵求意見。其中，人力資源和社會保障等部門要加強對煤礦企業勞動用工執法檢查。礦山安全監管監察部門要嚴厲整治違規轉包掛靠等問題，斬斷違法用工利益鏈條；公安、市場監管、稅務、能源等部門要嚴厲整治陰陽合同、計量造假、非法交易等問題，嚴查場外無票售煤、私設煤場囤銷、隱匿外銷回款、偷稅漏稅等行為，斬斷違法銷售利益鏈條；審計、金融監管等部門要嚴厲整治煤礦企業非法洗錢、資金體外迴圈、違規放貸借貸等問題，嚴查虛列工程套取資金、私戶發放黑工薪酬、賬外賬、“小金庫”、違規資金拆借等行為，斬斷違法金融利益鏈條；礦山安全監管監察、自然資源、住建、市場監管、能源等部門要嚴厲打擊第三方服務機構為煤礦非法違法生產提供監控系統、生產設施、圖紙設計、技術保障等服務行為，斬斷違法技術服務利益鏈條。

### 2026-07-21 13:44:58  #國際

【外媒稱美國政府考慮與聯合國難民署切斷關係】據外媒報道，美國特朗普政府近幾周正在考慮與聯合國難民署切斷關係，外交人員和聯合國官員正努力說服美國政府改變這一想法。 路透社20日援引多名匿名訊息人士的話報道說，美國與聯合國難民署近期在該機構副高階專員的任命問題上產生分歧，隨後開始考慮脫離該機構。今年6月，聯合國難民署任命美國職業外交官特雷莎·芬納蒂為副高階專員，而非特朗普政府支援的人選西蒙·漢金森。 數名訊息人士認為，美方原定上週就是否切斷關係做出決定，但至今仍未正式宣佈，可能表明外交遊說起了作用。 特朗普2025年重返白宮後，美國已宣佈退出包括世界衛生組織在內的多個聯合國機構。 難民署是聯合國重要機構，經聯合國大會一致同意於1950年12月14日成立，負責領導和協調國際行動，以保護全球難民及解決難民問題，總部設在日內瓦。由於人道主義援助資金大幅減少，該機構於2025年6月宣佈將精簡其總部及下屬機構，裁減約3500個職位。(新華社)

【山西：擬加強地方煤礦安全監督管理機構建設 改革煤礦安全監管人員派駐制度】《山西省統籌煤炭行業發展和安全新規十七條》即將釋出，現公開徵求意見。其中，擬加強地方煤礦安全監督管理機構建設。強化省地方煤礦安全監督管理部門對全省煤礦安全生產的監督管理、安全許可及業務指導工作，強化對煤礦企業貫徹執行安全生產法律法規及規程標準情況、安全生產條件、裝置設施安全情況的監管執法。各產煤市、產煤縣結合實際，最佳化煤礦安全監督管理局職能，加強安全風險實時監測預警能力建設和採礦、通風、地質等監測監管隊伍建設。同時，擬改革煤礦安全監管人員派駐制度。按照“國家指導、省級統籌、市級組建、縣級主用”的原則，實行煤礦安全派駐巡查制度，採取高風險特殊時段駐礦盯守、不放心礦井常態化派駐巡查等分級分類差異化監管模式，落實“駐礦吹哨、部門報到、接訴即辦”工作機制。實行派駐巡查全員短期輪崗制，同一駐礦安全監管組和同一駐礦安全監管人員在同一年度對同一煤礦連續派駐時間不得超過6個月。

### 2026-07-21 13:40:54  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy

【Shanxi to issue 17 new coal development and safety rules; will eliminate concealed working faces】Shanxi province will issue 17 new rules coordinating coal industry development and safety, including a complete ban on concealed working faces. Mines found to have hidden extraction points or falsified 'yin‑yang' mine plans will be ordered to suspend production for rectification and remove personnel and equipment from concealed underground sites; refusal to comply may trigger shutdowns, license revocations or permanent shaft sealing. Where conduct by legal representatives, actual controllers, responsible persons, ultimate beneficiaries, behind‑the‑scenes operators, mine managers or outsourced team leaders amounts to a crime, cases will be referred to judicial authorities. The rules require strict implementation of the 'three controls, three musts' and coal-mine safety responsibility lists and strengthen full‑chain safety supervision, enhancing the provincial emergency management department's oversight role. The Shanxi development and reform commission will review and approve mine-area master plans; the provincial development and reform commission and provincial energy bureau will approve coal development projects within their respective authorities.

Nickel Nears 1-Month High — Nickel traded around $17,000 per tonne, holding near one-month highs amid uncertainty over Indonesia’s production policies. Indonesia’s ongoing review of nickel production quotas remained a key focus, with the government limiting additional approvals to smelters facing raw material shortages, reinforcing expectations of tighter ore availability as policymakers seek to prevent oversupply and stabilize the market. At the same time, renewed Middle East tensions lifted oil prices and raised concerns over sulfur supplies, a key input for Indonesia’s nickel processing industry, increasing production cost pressures and adding to upward momentum in prices. Meanwhile, gains remained limited by weak downstream demand, with subdued activity in China’s stainless steel sector and cautious nickel sulfate purchases amid slower growth in nickel-based battery demand weighing on consumption prospects.

### 2026-07-21 13:27:08  #公司

【阿里巴巴集團旗下高德正式上線全新位置分享功能“位置口令”】近日，阿里巴巴集團旗下高德正式上線全新位置分享功能。使用者在高德App開啟該功能，選擇中國大陸及港澳臺地區的任意位置後，即可生成一組6位數字；接收方在高德“位置口令”頁面輸入這組數字後，便能直接獲取對應位置並一鍵導航。其中高德位置口令的本質，是將專業經緯度轉化為普通人也能直接使用和傳播的數字編碼。這讓地圖能力首次從“地點搜尋”延伸到了“任意位置表達”。使用者不再需要記住複雜經緯度，只需要6位數字，就能準確描述所在位置。位置口令如同空間世界裡的“二維碼”。正如二維碼降低了資訊獲取和傳播門檻，位置口令則降低了位置溝通的門檻，讓位置第一次像電話號碼一樣，可以被口頭傳播。

【美出臺原鋁關稅優惠彌補產能不足】美國白宮20日宣佈，美國總統特朗普當日簽署公告，依據《1962年貿易擴充套件法》第232條款等為在美國投資原鋁生產專案的企業提供原鋁進口關稅優惠。外媒分析認為，此舉旨在彌補美國原鋁產能不足。 公告說，美國商務部將建立機制，鼓勵在美投資新建、擴建或翻新鋁冶煉廠。符合條件的專案需要承諾在2029年1月20日前開工。美國商務部將允許專案所在企業每年進口與新專案預期年產量相同數量的原鋁，並按照《1962年貿易擴充套件法》第232條款適用的關稅減半繳納進口關稅。 去年6月，美國將進口自除英國外所有貿易伙伴的鋼鋁產品關稅從25%上調至50%。今年4月和6月，美國先後出臺政策，弱化鋼鋁關稅力度。彭博社報道說，美國政府原本希望透過50%的鋼鋁關稅提高美國國內產能，然而這一關稅措施成效有限。美國原鋁產能不足，嚴重依賴從加拿大和中東地區進口來滿足國內需求。(新華社)

The latest move signaled continued support for the currency, even as geopolitical tensions in the Middle East boosted demand for the US dollar as a safe-haven asset. Providing additional support, the State Council emphasized accelerating policy implementation and improving the efficiency of fiscal spending, underscoring its commitment to bolstering growth after Q2 GDP slowed to 4.3%, the weakest pace in more than three years and well below Beijing's annual target range of 4.5% to 5%. Rupiah Strengthens on BI Rate Hike Bets — The Indonesian rupiah firmed to around IDR 17,900 per U.S. dollar on Tuesday after approaching the 18,000 mark in the prior session, as traders positioned ahead of the central bank's policy meeting. Markets widely expect Bank Indonesia to lift its key rate by 25bps to 6.0% Wednesday as policymakers begin a two-day policy meeting today. Between May and June, the board raised borrowing costs by a cumulative 100bps to support the rupiah and financial stability. Meantime, the government has intensified inflation-control efforts after June's reading touched the upper end of the central bank's target of 1-1/2% to 3-1/2%, including moves to stabilise food prices. Gains were capped, however, by elevated oil prices that could add to fiscal strain. On the trade front, May marked the first trade deficit in six years, reducing support from external balances. Globally, the dollar index rose further as mounting U.S.–Iran tensions revived inflation concerns and reinforced views of policy tightening.

【韓元延長交易時段成交量激增 股市遭拋售帶動交投升溫】兩週前韓元啟動24小時不間斷外匯交易後，韓元單日成交量顯著走高，背後推手是外資持續拋售韓國本土晶片企業股票。首爾貨幣經紀公司與韓國貨幣經紀公司的資料顯示，7月6日至7月16日期間，美元兌韓元即期外匯日均交易額約186億美元。該數值較7月6日全天候交易落地前30天的日均規模高出約16%。統計資料未區分日間與夜間交易，且未計入韓國休市的7月17日。韓國官方此前預期全天候交易將提振韓元交易量，本次資料印證了這一判斷；而擴容外匯交易時段，是韓國爭取明晟（MSCI）發達市場評級的關鍵舉措。全新交易時長打破了持續數十年的嚴格外匯管控，但也引發市場擔憂：夜間流動性相對薄弱，或加劇匯率波動。7月初韓元一度走貶，跌至2009年以來低位，進一步放大了相關顧慮。紐約銀行亞太高階市場策略師Wee Khoon Chong表示，衡量24小時外匯交易機制是否成功的兩大核心指標為：盤後交易成交量能否持續提升，以及買賣價差能否收窄。韓國將韓元外匯交易時長由每日17小時延長至全天候24小時，此舉旨在提升海外投資者交易韓元的便利度。不過投資者仍需透過韓國本土境內銀行下達交易指令，只是訂單如今可全天任意時段處理。

【日本債市不安蓋過高市早苗首份經濟藍圖亮相】日本首相高市早苗在週二敲定的首份經濟藍圖中承諾將加大對增長領域的投資，但在市場擔憂政府可能干預貨幣政策、導致公債收益率攀升之際，這項承諾有所失色。高市早苗政府一直難以消除市場對其可能增加支出、並向日本央行施壓以推遲加息、從而遏制日本本已龐大債務融資成本的看法。隨著政府債券收益率自6月以來攀升至數十年來的高位，政府被迫多次調整該藍圖中關於貨幣政策的措辭。隨著長期利率上升，早期草案中呼籲實施“提振民間需求”貨幣政策的表述已被刪除。在後期版本將貨幣政策與政府提振經濟增長的舉措掛鉤、從而引發市場震動後，相關內容進行了修訂，以明確央行制定政策旨在“實現物價穩定上漲”。最終版綱要保留了敦促日本央行使其政策與政府政策保持一致的表述，但增設了一條腳註，援引法律中關於必須保護日本央行在制定政策方面獨立性的條款。“為了實現強勁的經濟，妥善實施貨幣政策以實現物價穩定上漲至關重要，”這份於週二獲得內閣批准的最終藍圖中寫道。

【CAAC to open international cargo corridors, bolster hub freight capacity during 15th Five-Year Plan】CAAC deputy director Han Jun told a State Council press briefing that during the 15th Five-Year Plan the authority will build a resilient, diversified and secure air cargo system. Priority measures include: opening international cargo corridor networks to raise transport efficiency and multi-route redundancy, strengthen links with emerging-market cargo hubs, and build an international route network coordinating freighter and belly capacity across traditional and emerging markets to expand global coverage; enhancing cargo functions at major hubs — notably Beijing, Shanghai, Guangzhou, Shenzhen and Chengdu — and scaling specialized cargo airports such as Ezhou, while supporting export-heavy provinces to lift international air‑cargo capacity via hub airports; upgrading airport cargo facility adaptability; cultivating diversified air-cargo firms and supporting Chinese freight carriers to scale up and transform into integrated logistics providers; and driving cost reduction and quality/efficiency improvements across air logistics.

Palladium Falls as Oil, Fed Bets Weigh — Palladium hovered around $1,260 per ounce, giving back recent gains as escalating tensions between the US and Iran lifted oil prices and reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve would keep interest rates elevated. Brent crude remained near a more than one-month high after Iran's Revolutionary Guards said they had struck US military assets across the Middle East, while Yemen's Iran-aligned Houthis declared a naval blockade against Saudi Arabia, fueling inflation concerns. Higher US Treasury yields and growing expectations of another Federal Reserve interest rate hike also added pressure to the broader precious metals complex, with traders raising the probability of a December rate increase to 83% from 73% a week earlier. Despite the latest moves, palladium remained down 22.81% year to date.

Palm Oil Dips on Profit-Taking — Malaysian palm oil futures slipped below MYR 4,650 per tonne, pulling back from recent gains as traders locked in profits after prices hit a near four-week high. Weaker edible oil prices on the Dalian and Chicago exchanges also weighed on sentiment. Meanwhile, softer crude oil prices reduced support for biofuel feedstocks, following reports of renewed U.S.-Iran mediation efforts. Demand worries added to the slide after June palm oil imports in top consumer India dropped to a 14-month low, with narrowing price discounts curbing buying. Still, losses were capped by supply risks: Malaysia’s meteorological agency warned of record-high temperatures next year as El Niño intensifies, threatening yields. The U.S. Climate Prediction Center likewise noted El Niño strengthened last month and is expected to persist through early 2027. Export data for July 1–20 were mixed, with AmSpec Agri Malaysia reporting a 0.9% decline from June, while Intertek Testing Services estimated shipments rose 4.1%.

Lagging sectors: oil & gas extraction, pharmaceuticals, CROs, power and coal mining. ### 2026-07-21 11:34:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Japan 10Y Yield Tracks US Treasury Yields Higher — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield rose to around 2.73% on Tuesday, extending recent gains and tracking a rise in US Treasury yields. The moves came as ongoing hostilities between the US and Iran pushed oil prices higher, reviving concerns about inflation and interest rate hikes. US attacks on Iran continued for a tenth straight day, with President Donald Trump warning that Tehran would be held responsible for the deaths of three US service members. With Japan heavily dependent on energy imports from the Middle East, the country remains especially vulnerable to supply disruptions. Markets are betting that higher energy costs could accelerate inflation in Japan and prompt a faster pace of Bank of Japan interest rate hikes. Additionally, the yen, which remained near a 40-year low against the dollar, could add to inflationary pressures and further strengthen the case for BOJ policy tightening.

### 2026-07-21 11:06:42  #其他

【國家鐵路局：我國鐵路網建設將圍繞三方面發力】國家鐵路局新聞發言人、副局長王啟銘在國新辦新聞釋出會上表示，今年上半年，鐵路固定資產投資繼續保持在高位，累計完成投資3632億元，同比增長了2.1%，為擴內需、穩增長髮揮了重要作用。下一步，我國鐵路網建設將圍繞三個方面發力。一是暢通道，加強戰略骨幹通道建設。強化沿江沿海沿邊通道佈局，推進跨區域戰略通道建設，加強跨江跨海跨灣通道建設，健全多元韌性的跨境鐵路通道體系，不斷提升鐵路戰略支撐保障能力。二是優結構，提升鐵路網路結構功能。加快貫通“八縱八橫”高鐵主通道，有序推進重點區域高鐵連線線建設，加強重點城市群都市圈通勤能力提升。補齊西部路網“留白”，強化能源糧食運輸通道建設，加快打通繁忙幹線運輸瓶頸，推動貨運網路能力提升。三是強節點，完善鐵路樞紐體系。加快推進北京等重點鐵路樞紐暢通工程設施建設，不斷提升樞紐韌性和靈活性。最佳化樞紐站場設施佈局，完善集疏運體系，加快推進網路融合、跨方式融合、站城融合發展。

### 2026-07-21 10:55:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Soybean Futures Hold Firm — Soybean futures held above $12.2 per bushel, staying near a nine-week high as renewed Chinese demand and higher crude oil prices outweighed better-than-expected US crop conditions. The USDA rated 66% of the nation's soybean crop in good-to-excellent condition, up one percentage point from the previous week and above market expectations. Meanwhile, ongoing fighting in the Middle East kept crude oil prices sharply higher, boosting the outlook for biofuel demand. Chinese buying also reinforced bullish sentiment, with the USDA reporting private export sales of 340,000 metric tons of US soybeans to China for delivery in the 2026/27 marketing year. However, China's June soybean imports from the US fell 20.6% year-on-year to 1.27 million tons, reflecting the impact of earlier trade tensions, while imports from Brazil rose 13.7% to 12.08 million tons, lifting China's total soybean arrivals to a record 13.55 million tons in June.

### 2026-07-21 10:53:45  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500

【Is Walsh's hawkishness a warning shot or preparation for real tightening?】New Fed chair Walsh has adopted an early hawkish tone to build anti-inflation credibility and respond to concerns about political interference. June nonfarm payrolls rose 57k and CPI fell 0.4% MoM, easing near-term pressure to tighten, but headline inflation remains 3.5% YoY—one weak print does not confirm a trend reversal. TS Lombard global macro managing director Dario Perkins says Walsh's hawkishness is not pure bravado: FOMC frustration over persistent inflation predated his arrival, and Middle East energy risk could push prices back up. The real policy divergence is over the next 6–12 months, not July or September. If US employment reaccelerates, it would indicate current policy is insufficiently tight and would undercut the view that AI-driven productivity can absorb demand, forcing Walsh to deliver sustained rate hikes to validate his stance.

【摩根大通戴蒙：市場低估風險 不會在當前價位買入股票或美國國債】摩根大通執行長傑米·戴蒙表示，投資者低估了全球經濟面臨的風險，並稱自己不會在當前價位買入股票或長期美國國債。在當地時間週一晚間釋出的一檔訪談中，戴蒙指出市場並未充分計入日益增多的地緣政治和財政威脅。"我確實認為這些風險可能比其他人認為的更大，"戴蒙表示，並指出烏克蘭和中東的戰爭，以及在政府赤字不斷攀升之際軍事開支的增加。當被問及市場是否低估了重大沖擊的可能性時，戴蒙表示，很難確切知道哪些風險已反映在資產價格中。“市場可能已經消化了某些因素，但真正尚未被消化的是實際會發生的事情。”他表示。當被問及是否會購買長期美國國債時，戴蒙表示："個人而言，我不會。"他對股市同樣持謹慎態度。戴蒙表示，雖然會考慮買入"優質投資標的"的個股，但不會在當前估值水平下買入整體市場。

【交通運輸部：“十五五”交通物流降成本持續發力，社會物流總費用與GDP比率已降至13.9%】7月21日，交通運輸部安全總監兼運輸服務司司長蔡團結在國新辦釋出會上表示，2025年交通運輸領域帶動降低物流成本，有力推動了社會物流總費用與GDP的比率降至了13.9%。“十五五”時期將加快推進物流網的建設，推動完善骨幹物流通道建設，深化重要樞紐節點的互聯互通，補充城鄉物流設施短板，加強國際物流設施佈局的建設。實施新一輪的國家綜合貨運樞紐補鏈強鏈提升行動。“十五五”時期將用三年左右的時間，集中力量再支援30個左右的城市補鏈強鏈，統籌硬投資和軟建設，推動設施裝備硬聯通和規則標準服務的軟聯通，進一步提升多式聯運功能，跨方式轉換效率。實施多式聯運攻堅行動。聚焦主要港口、重要內陸港、大型物流園區、規模化產業園區、中歐中亞班列集結中心等，打造1000個左右的多式聯運節點。首批將重點實施300個左右的主要貨運節點更新改造，著力提高多式聯運一小時的換裝率。推動交通物流數字化賦能，探索典型場景，貨車智慧駕駛等重大創新工程，規範網路貨運承運平臺的經營活動，謀劃開展無人快遞車規範應用的試點。培育交通物流的領軍企業，加快培育一批跨方式一體化專業化的交通物流領軍企業和綜合物流整合商。計劃到2030年，力爭培育100家左右的綜合物流整合商。

Traders also welcomed the proposed Indonesia International Financial Center (PFII), designed to handle foreign currency transactions and operate in English, underscoring ambitions to position Jakarta as a regional hub. 【Chip sell-off pauses; tech giants' earnings will be the real test】Overnight US chip stocks halted declines, with a semiconductor ETF up about 1.3% and gains in NVIDIA, AMD and Micron; the Nasdaq was near unchanged. Earlier concentrated selling and leverage-driven rebalancing pressures have eased temporarily. US stabilization gives a brief reprieve to Korean and broader East Asian memory and equipment chains, but deleveraging inertia and high-volatility positions remain unresolved. Upcoming results from ALPHABET, Tesla and Intel will re-test AI demand, capex plans and earnings delivery; whether the rebound holds depends on whether results can justify prior elevated expectations.

### 2026-07-21 10:10:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Platinum Trades Near Multi-Month Lows — Platinum futures fell below $1,600 an ounce, near late-November lows as the ongoing US-Iran conflict heightened the risk of energy-driven inflation and higher interest rates. The resulting surge in oil prices from the ongoing conflict pushed US Treasury yields higher and prompted markets to increase the odds of a Federal Reserve rate hike in September to about 55%, weighing on non-yielding metals such as platinum. Meanwhile, Sibanye-Stillwater plans to advance seven PGM mining projects, although production is not expected to begin until next year. The World Platinum Investment Council expects a fourth consecutive market deficit in 2026, as constrained mine supply, elevated energy costs, and higher winter electricity tariffs continue to pressure producers' margins. China's industrial policies supporting AI, electric vehicles, and clean energy, together with the launch of the first platinum investment bar series, are also expected to support long-term platinum consumption.

【瑞銀：升海螺水泥評級至“中性” 降目標價至18.2港元】瑞銀髮布研報稱，中國水泥行業僅處於結構性需求下行週期的中段，但見底證據日益明顯，預期行業的盈利能力將在今年見底，並自2027年起輕微復甦。該行考慮到海螺水泥(00914)的估值溢價已大致變得正常，風險回報更趨平衡，故將其評級由“沽售”上調至“中性”，但目標價由21.4港元下調至18.2港元。該行將海螺水泥2026及27年盈利預測下調16%及5%，分別至71億及75億元人民幣。該行又將PO-42.5水泥平均售價預測下調至每噸345元人民幣，以反映低價競爭持續，但預期動力煤價格將由今年的每噸750元人民幣降至2027年的720元人民幣，相信燃料成本下降有助利潤逐步回升。

Hong Kong Stocks Flat Ahead of Inflation Data — The Hang Seng Index traded flat at 25,133 on Tuesday as investors remained on the sidelines ahead of Hong Kong's inflation data due later in the day. Market participants continued to monitor escalating hostilities in the Middle East, which lifted oil prices and renewed concerns over inflation and the prospect of further interest rate hikes. The downside was limited as investors continued to draw support from Beijing's recent efforts to stabilize the stock market. Optimism was also aided by signs of improving conditions in Hong Kong's commercial property market after a major office tower owned by CK Asset Holdings, controlled by Li Ka-shing, began attracting tenants following a prolonged period of low occupancy. Among notable movers were Z.AI Co. (9.7%), Tencent (0.9%), Semiconductor Manufacturing Corporation (1.5%), Lenovo (2.2%), and Hong Kong Exchanges and Clearing (1.1%).

China Stocks Gain on Tech Boost — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.4% to 3,812 on Tuesday, while the Shenzhen Component gained 0.7%, as pressure on technology stocks eased following a recent sharp selloff. Investors turned their attention to upcoming earnings from major global technology companies for fresh clues on the sustainability of the AI-driven market rally. Supporting sentiment, Jijia Vision is in talks to launch GigaAI's IPO in Hong Kong as early as this year, adding to the growing pipeline of Chinese AI firms seeking public listings. Several domestic AI startups, including DeepSeek, Moonshot AI, and 01.ai, are also reportedly exploring capital market debuts as they compete with leading US developers and accelerate the commercialization of their technologies. Technology and AI-related stocks led the gains, with Cambricon Technologies rising 1.6%, Hygon Information Technology advancing 5.0%, Zhongji Innolight adding 2.5%, and Eoptolink Technology climbing 5.6%.

### 2026-07-21 09:54:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

EU Gas Prices Ease on Hopes for US-Iran De-Escalation — European natural gas prices declined 2% to around €57.5 per MWh on Tuesday after reaching a four-month high in the previous session, as markets assessed reports of mediation efforts to ease tensions between the US and Iran. Tehran said it had received a proposal from mediators for a 10-day ceasefire aimed at reviving the interim deal reached last month, offering some hope for a potential de-escalation of the conflict. This comes as the US carried out a tenth consecutive day of strikes against Iran, while Tehran continued retaliatory attacks against neighboring countries. Shipping through Hormuz has largely stalled and concerns over the safety of commercial vessels have continued to grow following Iranian attacks over the weekend. The conflict has also delayed the anticipated recovery in Qatari LNG exports, making it more difficult for Europe to rebuild gas inventories ahead of winter, with storage facilities currently less than 54% full, compared with 64% at the same time last year.

South Korean Shares Rebound — The benchmark KOSPI rose more than 3% to around 6,720 on Tuesday, rebounding from an almost three-month low as bargain hunting emerged in beaten-down technology stocks following last week's sharp selloff. Samsung Electronics and SK Hynix led the recovery, advancing over 4% and 3%, respectively. At the same time, South Korea's exports surged 52.3% in the first 20 days of July, fueled by a 180% jump in semiconductor shipments amid sustained AI demand, further boosting confidence in the sector's earnings outlook. Other notable advancers included SK Square (3.0%), Kia Corporation (1.1%), Shinhan Financial Group (2.2%), Hanwha Aerospace (4.1%), SK Inc. (1.4%), and HD Hyundai Electric (1.3%). However, the rebound was tempered by lingering concerns over escalating tensions in the Middle East, as rising oil prices and weaker sentiment on Wall Street continued to weigh on global risk appetite.

### 2026-07-21 09:41:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Rises to 1-Month High — The Australian dollar rose to around $0.70, hitting more than a four-week high as the ongoing US-Iran conflict raised the risk of energy-driven inflation, fueling expectations that the Reserve Bank of Australia could deliver another interest rate hike. US attacks on Iran entered a tenth consecutive day, while Iran-backed Houthi militants also announced a maritime embargo against Saudi Arabia, raising fears of broader disruptions to global energy supplies. The resulting surge in oil prices reinforced expectations that the Reserve Bank of Australia could tighten policy further after raising the cash rate three times to 4.35%, with markets pricing a 76% chance of another increase by December. Investors are also awaiting Australia's June employment report on Thursday, with forecasts pointing to a 15,000 rise in employment and an unchanged 4.4% unemployment rate. A weaker-than-expected labor market reading could prompt traders to scale back expectations for further policy tightening.

### 2026-07-21 09:40:11  #公司 #市場

【樂鑫科技：有望獲得更多成本敏感型專案的匯入機會】樂鑫科技近日接受機構調研時表示，公司認為，儲存器價格上漲對公司的直接影響相對有限。一方面，公司產品主要搭配NOR Flash，相關儲存成本在整體方案中的佔比相對較低，因此成本波動整體可控。另一方面，從市場角度來看，儲存器漲價可能推動部分終端產品重新評估硬體架構。一些原本採用Linux搭配NAND Flash、DDR的方案，在成本上升後，可能更傾向於採用RTOS搭配NOR Flash的輕量化方案，在滿足功能需求的同時實現更優的成本控制。公司長期深耕RTOS平臺，在軟體生態、開發工具和應用積累方面具有較好的基礎，因此有望受益於這一趨勢，獲得更多成本敏感型專案的匯入機會。總體來看，公司認為這一變化更多體現為產品結構和方案選擇的調整，為公司進一步擴大AIoT市場覆蓋範圍提供了新的機會。

【韓國電商巨頭物流中心大火連燒3天 面臨垮塌風險】韓國電商巨頭酷澎公司一處物流中心18日燃起大火，火情持續3天仍未被完全控制。當地消防部門20日警告，如果火勢蔓延至存有大量鋰電池的區域，整個樓體將面臨垮塌風險。為防止險情發生，政府部門已組織附近居民疏散，並要求學校停課。這一物流中心位於首爾以西的仁川市，18日6時54分起火。根據當天釋出的國家消防動員令，當地已部署813名消防員和231件裝置參與滅火。消防部門官員20日說，火情雖得到一定控制，但將火完全撲滅所需時間“比預想要長”，加上該物流中心規模較大，目前無法預計完全控制火情的具體時間。（新華社）

Gasoline Holds Near 2-Month Highs — US gasoline prices traded around $3.38 per gallon, remaining near the highest level in two months, as markets weighed renewed diplomatic efforts between the US and Iran against escalating military exchanges. Iran said it had received a 10-day ceasefire proposal from mediators aimed at preserving the interim agreement and advancing a lasting peace deal. The diplomatic push followed another night of US strikes on Iranian cities and Iranian attacks on US military assets across the region. Amid the tensions, shipping activity through the Strait of Hormuz came close to a standstill. Adding to supply concerns, Yemen's Houthi militants threatened to blockade shipping to and from Saudi Arabia through the Red Sea. Meanwhile, Ukrainian attacks on Russian energy infrastructure continued to disrupt refinery operations, while stronger diesel and jet fuel margins prompted refiners to curb gasoline output. At the pump, average US gasoline prices climbed back above $4 per gallon.

【Z.ai commissions 1GW domestic AI compute centre, completes acquisition of XCore Sigma】Z.ai has commissioned a 1GW-class domestic AI compute data centre that uses only domestic AI chips, and today completed the acquisition of XCore Sigma, a heterogeneous AI compute software firm spun out of the Institute of Computing Technology at the Chinese Academy of Sciences. XCore Sigma specialises in heterogeneous compute software stacks and compiler optimisation and is regarded as one of China’s leading AI infra teams, sources said. The two moves respectively address compute supply and compute utilisation: the data centre expands large-model training capacity, while XCore Sigma’s compiler, runtime and inference-engine capabilities aim to raise heterogeneous-chip utilisation, lower inference costs and speed model deployment.

U.S. to Hit Canada with Broad 50% Tariffs — Canada faces the threat of steep new U.S. trade measures after the Trump administration on Monday announced plans to impose a 50% tariff on Canadian imports starting in 30 days, accusing Ottawa of discriminating against U.S. exports, including alcohol, automobiles, and dairy products. The White House said the tariffs would cover a broad range of goods, from hockey sticks to cement, and would also apply to products covered under the U.S.-Mexico-Canada Agreement (USMCA) that had been exempt from previous tariff actions. Only a limited number of products would be spared, including energy, critical minerals, potash, fish, and goods already subject to sector-specific Section 232 tariffs. The move marks a significant escalation in trade tensions between the two neighboring economies and could disrupt cross-border supply chains if implemented.

Australia Shares Retreat to Near 2-Week Low — Australian equities fell 47 points or 0.5% to 8,744 on Tuesday morning deals, extending weakness for the fourth straight session and approaching their lowest level in nearly two weeks. Sentiment tracked weaker U.S. futures after Wall Street closed lower overnight, pressured by rising oil and Treasury yields. Geopolitical tensions added to caution as President Trump vowed Iran would “pay” for the deaths of three U.S. service members, while Yemen’s Houthis declared a maritime embargo on Saudi Arabia. Locally, traders stayed guarded ahead of June labor data and July flash PMIs later this week. Most sectors dropped, led by manufacturing, healthcare, logistics, and process industries. The big four banks lost 0.9–1.2%, while Pro Medicus (-3.6%), QBE (-2.1%), Brambles (-1.9%), and Lynas (-1.0%) weighed. Bucking the trend, Yancoal Australia jumped 3.9% while South32 was up 3.7%. Nextdc rose 2.6% after reporting an 11% rise in contracted utilisation over the final ten weeks of its fiscal year.

New Zealand Dollar Rises After Hot Inflation Data — The New Zealand dollar rose to $0.585, an over six-week high, after higher-than-expected inflation data reinforced expectations of further monetary tightening by the Reserve Bank. Annual inflation accelerated to 4.1% in the second quarter from 3.1% in the previous quarter, exceeding both market forecasts of 4.0% and the RBNZ's latest projection of 3.9%. It also marked the highest level since the fourth quarter of 2023 and was well above the central bank's 1–3% target range. The hot print cemented bets that the RBNZ will deliver another rate hike in September, with markets also pricing in additional increases in either October or December, followed by another move in February next year. The prospect of higher domestic interest rates has continued to underpin the kiwi, helping it outperform many of its peers despite a broader deterioration in global risk sentiment caused by escalating tensions in the Middle East.

【受人工智慧相關產品拉動，韓國7月前20天出口創歷年7月新高】得益於人工智慧催生的半導體需求持續拉動經濟，韓國7月上旬出口延續強勁增勢。韓國海關週二公佈的資料顯示，經工作日調整後，7月前20天出口額同比大漲62.9%，創下歷年7月同期出口最高紀錄；上月同期該增幅為49.7%。未經調整口徑下，出口同比增長52.3%，進口同比上漲20%，實現122億美元貿易順差。資料顯示，在人工智慧與資料中心持續投資的支撐下，韓國外需保持韌性。半導體持續成為韓國出口與整體經濟增長的核心引擎，一定程度對沖了中東局勢帶來的負面影響。晶片出口同比飆升180.6%，計算機相關產品出口增幅接近232%，燃料出口上漲33.4%；汽車出口同比下滑10.6%。亮眼的外貿資料印證了韓國央行上週加息的決策——該行時隔三年多首次上調政策利率。全體委員一致將基準利率上調25個基點至2.75%，並釋放持續收緊貨幣政策的訊號：當前通脹仍高於政策目標，且經濟增速有望超出央行此前預測。

【受油價飆升推動，紐西蘭第二季度通脹急升 汽油價格上漲的貢獻佔了近四分之一】受燃料成本飆升推動，紐西蘭消費者物價在第二季度大幅上漲，加劇了有關利率需在未來數月大幅上調的擔憂。StatsNZ週二公佈的資料顯示，消費者價格指數(CPI)第二季度環比上漲1.5%，截至6月底同比上漲4.1%。第一季度的同比通脹率為3.1%。目前的通脹率是紐西蘭央行目標水平的兩倍多。與此同時，該央行高階官員警告稱，企業似乎越來越傾向於將成本迅速轉嫁給消費者。雖然油價已從2月和3月的高位回落，但近期中東地區戰火重燃再度推高油價，令紐西蘭面臨又一輪國際衝擊的風險。經濟學家還估計，紐西蘭央行(RBNZ)目前2.50%的官方隔夜拆款利率可能比中性利率低多達75個基點，這意味著該央行在為通脹降溫方面仍有更多工作要做。對同比通脹率貢獻最大的是汽油價格，該價格當季躍升27.5%。汽油價格在第二季度上漲了20.1%。資料顯示，在CPI 4.1%的同比漲幅中，汽油價格上漲的貢獻佔了近四分之一。StatsNZ稱，第二季度柴油價格上漲71%，紐西蘭家庭在汽油上的支出約是柴油的八倍。

NZX 50 Flat After Inflation Data — The NZX 50 was almost flat, trading around 13,697 in Tuesday morning trade, remaining muted for the second straight session, as gains in healthcare, technology, and financials offset declines in communication services, industrials, and consumer staples. Traders assessed fresh data, as inflation accelerated in Q2 to the highest level since Q4 2023, while also evaluating its impact on the central bank's interest rate decision at the upcoming meeting following a 25-bps rate hike earlier this month. Caution also prevailed amid persistently high oil prices due to escalating tensions in the Middle East, as inflation concerns fueled expectations of further interest rate hikes. RBNZ Chief Economist Paul Conway recently warned that sticky inflation could prompt further interest rate hikes. Ebos Group rose 0.6%, Fisher & Paykel Healthcare gained 0.8%, and Mercury NZ climbed 0.6%, while Chorus, Colonial Motor Company, and Auckland International Airport fell 2.3%, 1.4%, and 1.3%, respectively

### 2026-07-21 06:53:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

New Zealand Q2 Inflation Rate Highest Since 2023 — Annual inflation in New Zealand accelerated to 4.1% in Q2 2026, from 3.1% in Q1, and was above forecasts of 4.0%, surpassing the RBNZ’s 1–3% target range. It was the highest inflation rate since Q4 2023. The largest contributor was the transport group, where prices jumped 10.6% (vs. 3.3% in Q1), mainly driven by petrol (up 20.1%). On a quarterly basis, the CPI rose 1.5% in Q2, accelerating from a 0.9% increase in the previous quarter, above market estimates of 1.4% and marking the highest level since Q3 2023.

A former analyst said: In the past people focused on whether reports were right; now attacks target the analyst personally rather than the content. He added: Especially in volatile markets, retail backlash is stronger, making it harder for analysts to remain dispassionate.

### 2026-07-21 05:08:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Argentina Trade Surplus Widens in June — Argentina's trade surplus widened to $2.19 billion in June 2026 from $0.88 billion a year earlier, marking its 31st consecutive month of positive trade balances. Exports rose 24.5% year-on-year to $9.06 billion, with growth broad-based across all major categories. Agricultural manufactures surged 31.6%, driven by higher shipments of fats and oils, food industry residues and waste, and meat products. Industrial manufactures also advanced 31.4%, supported mainly by chemicals and related products, as well as base metals and metal products. Exports of fuels and energy climbed 31.1%, as higher international prices more than offset lower shipment volumes. Imports increased 7.3% to $6.86 billion, driven primarily by fuels and lubricants, whose purchases soared 126.3%. Imports of intermediate goods rose 9.5%, while consumer goods increased 3.3%. In contrast, capital goods fell 8.0%, parts and accessories for capital goods declined 9.4%, and motor vehicle imports dropped 10.6%.

### 2026-07-21 04:47:59  #MKT News

【Korean equities volatile as KRX launches HFT impact study】KRX is soliciting bids for a research project to assess the market impact of a surge in high-frequency trading (HFT) and to explore regulatory options, the exchange said on the 20th. Bids are due on the 27th and the study is slated for completion in H2. HFT uses sub-second, fully automated order flow and is heavily used by foreign institutional investors for latency arbitrage; its profitability rises as securities transaction taxes fall. Concerns that increased HFT could disrupt markets have persisted since South Korea cut the securities transaction tax in 2019. KRX said the study will inform a regulatory framework; existing risk controls include a 2023 registration regime for high-speed algorithmic traders and a batch-order cancellation circuit breaker.

### 2026-07-21 03:28:42  #MKT News #XAUUSD #Impact bullish #WTI #DXY

【Expert: US lacks capability to destroy Iran】Veteran war reporter Elijah Magnier said the US can mount high‑intensity strikes on Iranian facilities but, at best, would only weaken Iran. He noted the US has large offensive munitions stocks but limited interceptor missile inventories, constraining its ability to defend US assets, allies and Israel from retaliatory strikes. Sustaining a large campaign would force hard choices about which bases, cities and incoming threats to protect. Magnier said Iran is likely to broaden the geographic scope of any conflict to increase the number of targets Washington must defend, raising the political — and especially economic — cost of further strikes. By using concentrated ballistic and cruise‑missile saturation attacks to wear down air defenses and force interceptors onto decoy targets, Iran could target US regional bases, logistics hubs, ports, radar facilities and fuel storage. Strikes on Gulf allies could increase domestic pressure on those governments to push the US to de‑escalate.

【多位券商首席談股市：企穩反彈條件已具備 A股市場中長期韌性充足】近期A股市場出現階段性調整，多位券商首席經濟學家及首席分析師表示，近期調整受外部輸入性因素傳導，屬於技術性回撥，當前市場已具備反彈條件且A股中長期韌性充足。中國銀河證券首席經濟學家、研究院院長章俊表示，近期美聯儲官員鷹派表態推升美債收益率與美元指數，壓制全球高估值成長股；美股、日韓科技股集體大跌，儲存產業鏈恐慌情緒跨境傳導，北向資金階段性流出，疊加地緣因素升溫、原油價格抬升，屬於外圍情緒被動傳導。中信建投證券首席經濟學家黃文濤稱，“短期衝擊可能改變交易節奏，但無法改變國內政策方向和科技產業趨勢。海外市場本輪下跌消化的是集中度和槓桿，而A股在流動性環境、交易擁擠度、上漲驅動力等方面與之存在結構性區別。與海外相比，A股承受了與自身風險結構並不完全匹配的折價。”在川財證券首席經濟學家、研究所所長陳靂看來，國內宏觀基本面、產業發展邏輯並未發生趨勢性弱化，經濟託底政策持續落地，製造業盈利修復，未來產業培育穩步推進。(上證報)

Brent Back to $89 — Brent rose to a five-week high of around $89 per barrel on Monday after a volatile session in which prices swung between gains and losses. The rally gathered pace after US President Trump warned that Iran would be held responsible for the deaths of three US service members, while Yemen's Houthi militants announced a maritime embargo targeting Saudi Arabia, heightening concerns over Red Sea energy shipments. Supply risks also remained elevated following recent attacks on oil tankers and energy facilities in Kuwait. Meanwhile, Iran said mediators had presented proposals aimed at easing tensions with the US after several days of hostilities. The conflict has seen the US carry out airstrikes on Iran for nine consecutive nights, while Iran has retaliated with missile attacks on US allies in the Middle East. Earlier in the session, an Iranian Foreign Ministry spokesman said negotiations with the US could resume, provided they align with Tehran's national interests.

【Burnham names cabinet; John Healey appointed chancellor】On the 20th, King Charles III commissioned new Prime Minister Burnham to form a government and Burnham appointed his cabinet. John Healey, who served as defence secretary in the Starmer government and resigned in June over defence spending disputes, was named chancellor. Ed Miliband was appointed foreign secretary; he led Labour from 2010–15 and served as energy secretary under Starmer. Louise Haigh was named First Secretary of State and Minister for the Duchy of Lancaster; she previously served as transport secretary and resigned in November 2024 and is viewed as a key Burnham ally. US Stocks Trim Gains — US stocks trimmed early gains and traded mixed on Monday as escalating geopolitical tension weighed against fresh support from chip producers. The S&P 500 and the Nasdaq 100 were slightly higher, while the Dow fell over 200 points. Long-term Treasury yields rose after the US and Iran extended strikes on Monday, while Houthi forces stated they would attack commercial vessels from Saudi Arabia in the Red Sea. Fuel costs remained elevated and risked pro-inflationary pressures that support the outlook of a Fed rate hike this year. Apple and Oracle dropped around the 3% level, while Morgan Stanley and Goldman Sachs erased early gains to fall over 1% each. Still, chip producers gained as markets continued to gauge whether AI hyperscalers are due to slow spending on AI infrastructure in the next quarters. Micron and Sandisk rose 5%. Alphabet gained 2% ahead of its guidance update on Wednesday, the latest bellwether for both chip and software development capital expenditure.

### 2026-07-21 01:06:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Canadian Bond Yield Edges Higher — The yield on Canada's 10-year government bond rose above 3.57% in July, the highest in two months, as investors weighed rising US Treasury yields against soft Canadian inflation data. US bond yields increased as tensions between the US and Iran escalated over the weekend, sending oil prices higher before they pared most of their gains following comments from Iran's foreign ministry indicating that negotiations with the US could continue. Meanwhile, Canada's annual inflation rate eased to 2.8% in June 2026 from 3.2% in May, slightly below forecasts of 2.9%. Gasoline prices increased at a slower pace, while the Bank of Canada's preferred core inflation measures fell to their lowest levels in more than five years, reinforcing the BoC's view that the impact of higher energy costs stemming from the Middle East oil supply crisis is not spreading broadly throughout the economy. The softer inflation data reduced expectations of further Bank of Canada interest rate hikes this year.

### 2026-07-21 00:29:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Currency #Importance 1

Canadian Dollar Slips on Cooling Inflation — The Canadian dollar weakened to around 1.41 per USD in July, pulling back from a near one-month high following the release of June's inflation report. Canada's annual inflation rate eased to 2.8% in June 2026 from 3.2% in May, slightly below forecasts of 2.9%. Gasoline prices increased at a slower pace, while the Bank of Canada's preferred core inflation measures fell to their lowest levels in more than five years, reinforcing the BoC's view that the impact of higher energy costs stemming from the Middle East oil supply crisis is not spreading broadly throughout the economy. The softer inflation data reduced expectations of further Bank of Canada interest rate hikes this year, lowering the relative yield that attracts foreign capital. At its latest meeting, the Bank of Canada kept its key policy rate unchanged at 2.25%, as widely expected.

FTSE MIB Ends Flat — The FTSE MIB ended little changed at 51,863 on Monday as traders remained cautious ahead of a busy earnings week. Escalating tensions in the Middle East kept risk sentiment subdued, although markets drew some optimism from reports of a proposed 10-day pause in hostilities. Meanwhile, rising European natural gas prices increased concerns over lower profit margins, weighing on utility stocks, with Terna (-1.2%) and A2A (-0.9%) among the sector's biggest decliners. Avio led losses on the index, falling 2.8%, followed by Telecom Italia (-2.4%) and Inwit (-1.7%). On the upside, Moncler jumped 3.2% ahead of its earnings release, with revenue expected to rise 4.5%. Amplifon (2.6%), Saipem (2.6%), Nexi (2.1%), and STMicroelectronics (2.1%) also advanced, with the latter rebounding from recent losses. Financials were also higher as investors expected the ECB to leave interest rates unchanged at this week's meeting. Banco BPM rose 1.0%, while UniCredit gained 0.6% ahead of its earnings report.

Meanwhile, an ECB survey showed euro area firms expect slower growth in selling prices and wages, reinforcing evidence that the recent energy-driven inflation surge has yet to trigger meaningful second-round price effects. Financials advanced, with BNP Paribas up 1.1%, Crédit Agricole adding 0.7%, and Société Générale gaining 0.7%. Schneider Electric rose 1.1% and STMicroelectronics added 2.1%, tracking a rebound in US tech stocks. Luxury shares traded mixed as risk aversion rose on escalating US-Iran tensions, with LVMH (-1.4%) and L'Oréal (-0.2%) down, while Hermès (+0.2%) and Kering (+0.6%) rose. Brent Crude Holds At $88 — Brent crude oil steadied around $88 per barrel on Monday, hovering at a five-week high, after swinging sharply between gains and losses as traders weighed escalating Middle East tensions against renewed diplomatic efforts. Yemen's Iran-backed Houthi rebels announced a ban on maritime traffic from Saudi Arabia, raising concerns over Red Sea energy shipments, while recent attacks on oil tankers and facilities in Kuwait kept supply risks elevated. At the same time, reports suggested mediators had proposed a 10-day pause in hostilities between the US and Iran to revive their fragile interim peace agreement. Iran also confirmed it had received diplomatic proposals aimed at easing the conflict. Still, shipping through the Strait of Hormuz remained severely disrupted after fresh attacks on commercial vessels. Beyond the Middle East, supply concerns intensified after drone strikes again halted oil loading at Russia's Caspian Pipeline Consortium terminal on the Black Sea.

Swiss 10-Year Yield Near 2-Month High — Switzerland's 10-year government bond yield rose above 0.45%, further increasing to a near two-month high, as escalating tensions in the Middle East lifted inflation expectations. Oil prices surged amid intensifying hostilities between the US and Iran, raising energy costs and prompting markets to reassess the outlook for inflation, economic growth, and monetary policy. The Swiss National Bank left its key policy rate unchanged at 0% at its latest meeting, expecting inflation to remain little changed in the medium term. However, meeting minutes highlighted that policymakers see rising geopolitical tensions as a near-term inflation risk. The SNB also reiterated its willingness to intervene in the foreign exchange market to counter excessive franc appreciation and preserve price stability, while the IMF urged the central bank to remain cautious should a stagflation scenario emerge.

### 2026-07-20 22:47:46  #MKT News #Macro & Rates

【Goldman Sachs: Turkey may tolerate faster lira depreciation】Goldman Sachs economists expect Turkey may allow a faster depreciation of the Turkish lira to prioritize external balance stability over keeping inflation suppression as the top priority. Clemens Grafe and Basak Edizgil say the lira could depreciate at an annualized rate of more than 20% against the dollar, even if that slows disinflation. They add that faster currency weakness implies policy rates will need to remain higher than current market pricing to preserve financial stability amid ongoing de-dollarization. Turkey’s disinflation strategy partly relies on a real appreciation of the lira — i.e. nominal depreciation slower than inflation — to relieve price pressures. The lira has fallen about 9% versus the dollar year-to-date.

### 2026-07-20 22:31:46  #市場 #央行 #國際

【美債期貨市場倉位分化：5年期合約遭大幅平倉，資金向8月聯邦基金期貨集中 】據芝加哥商品交易所（CME）公佈的最新未平倉合約資料顯示，美債及利率期貨市場正經歷顯著的倉位調整，不同期限合約的未平倉量呈現明顯的分化態勢。在國債期貨方面，5年期美國國債合約連續第二個交易日遭遇平倉，未平倉合約量大幅下降約3.4萬張（相當於每基點下降約150萬美元），創下6月30日以來的最大平倉幅度。與此同時，2年期國債合約未平倉量已連續第四個交易日下降，四個交易日累計降幅相當於每基點下降約500萬美元。相比之下，10年期和超10年期國債的未平倉合約量則出現了增加。在利率衍生品市場，大多數SOFR（有擔保隔夜融資利率）期貨期限的未平倉合約均出現下降，其中2027年到期的四份合約每基點下跌約160萬美元。而在聯邦基金期貨方面，7月合約的未平倉合約量下降近2.1萬份，跌至6月23日以來的最低水平；與之形成鮮明對比的是，8月合約的未平倉合約量延續了上升勢頭，目前已接近80萬份，是7月合約的兩倍多。這一倉位變動表明，隨著7月合約到期臨近，交易員正加速平倉或移倉。資金正顯著向8月聯邦基金期貨集中，反映出市場對美聯儲8月利率決議的高度關注與博弈正在升溫。

【KPMG: Canada's energy-cost pass-through to consumer prices not yet evident】KPMG Canada chief economist Ali Jaffery said June CPI shows limited evidence that rising energy costs have transmitted to food and other goods. Headline inflation slowed to 2.8% in June from 3.2% in May. Jaffery noted academic literature suggests such pass-through can take a year or more to peak, so the slow start is encouraging, and said concerns that higher energy prices combined with U.S. tariffs are accelerating goods inflation are not borne out. Excluding travel services the CPI basket shows "considerable weakness"; travel services rose in June amid World Cup matches in Toronto and Vancouver.

Dollar Steady on Monday — The dollar index hovered around 101 on Monday, as investors continued to assess developments in the Middle East and their implications for the economic and monetary policy outlook. Tensions between the US and Iran escalated over the weekend, sending oil prices to six-week highs before they pared most of their gains following comments from Iran's foreign ministry indicating that negotiations with the US could continue if the country's national interests are respected. Last week, both CPI and PPI reports came in softer than expected, but the recent surge in oil prices has raised concerns that the disinflation trend could stall. Markets continue to price in one Fed rate hike this year, with the probability of a September move remaining above 60%. Meanwhile, Fed officials have entered the blackout period ahead of next week's FOMC meeting, where policymakers are widely expected to leave the federal funds rate unchanged.

Treasury Yields Higher on Monday — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note edged up to 4.57% on Monday, after declining about 7bps the previous week, as investors continued to assess developments in the Middle East and their implications for the economic and monetary policy outlook. Tensions between the US and Iran escalated over the weekend, sending oil prices to six-week highs before they pared most of their gains following comments from Iran's foreign ministry indicating that negotiations with the US could continue if the country's national interests are respected. Last week, both CPI and PPI reports came in softer than expected, but the recent surge in oil prices has raised concerns that the disinflation trend could stall. Markets continue to price in one Fed rate hike this year, with the probability of a September move remaining above 60%. Meanwhile, Fed officials have entered the blackout period ahead of next week's FOMC meeting, where policymakers are widely expected to leave the federal funds rate unchanged.

### 2026-07-20 21:55:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Stock Market #Importance 1

TSX Edges Higher on Softer Inflation Data — The S&P/TSX Composite Index inched up to trade above 35,000 on Monday following the release of June's inflation report. Canada's annual inflation rate eased to 2.8% in June 2026 from 3.2% in May, slightly below forecasts of 2.9%. Gasoline prices increased at a slower pace, while the Bank of Canada's preferred core inflation measures fell to their lowest levels in more than five years, reinforcing the BoC's view that the impact of higher energy costs stemming from the Middle East oil supply crisis is not spreading broadly throughout the economy. Canadian bond yields moved lower following the data release. Most financials traded higher, with TD Bank, BMO, CIBC, and Scotiabank edging up. Shopify (+1%) and Celestica (+4%) advanced, tracking the rebound in US technology shares. Energy stocks weighed on the index, with Canadian Natural and Suncor down about 1% each as oil prices pulled back from recent peaks.

Platinum Steadies Near Eight-Month Low — Platinum futures traded near $1,610 per ounce, stabilizing around their lowest since November, as traders weighed the metal's supply and demand outlook against renewed tensions in the Middle East. Rising inflation concerns prompted markets to price in at least one Federal Reserve rate hike by year-end, weighing on demand for non-yielding metals such as platinum. Meanwhile, Sibanye-Stillwater plans to advance seven PGM mining projects, although production is not expected to begin until next year. However, the World Platinum Investment Council expects a fourth consecutive market deficit in 2026, as constrained mine supply, elevated energy costs, and higher winter electricity tariffs continue to pressure producers' margins. On the demand side, China's industrial policies supporting AI, electric vehicles, and clean energy, together with the launch of the first platinum investment bar series by Caibai and the WPIC, are expected to support long-term platinum consumption.

### 2026-07-20 21:47:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ethiopia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Ethiopia Inflation Rate at 1-Year High — The annual inflation rate in Ethiopia rose to 13.9% in June 2026, marking the highest level in a year and accelerating from 13.4% in the previous month. Food inflation accelerated to 15.1% from 15% in May, the highest level since January 2025, due to increases in most food commodities such as bread and cereals (7.3%) and vegetables (12.8%), while costs for meat products eased to 19.2%. Non-food inflation also accelerated, to 12.2% from 11.1%. Housing and utilities costs rose further (9.5% vs 6.8%), along with restaurants and hotels (12% vs 10.5%), furnishings (13% vs 12.7%), clothing and footwear (13.5% vs 13.2%) and education (17.3% vs 9.6%). In contrast, inflation eased for transports (14.3% vs 17.1%) and alcoholic beverges and tobacco (14.5% vs 14.6%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1% in June, easing from a 1.7% increase in the previous month.

The 2026 IPCA forecast was revised down to 5.15% from 5.16% a week earlier and 5.33% one month ago. The market also maintained its forecast for the benchmark Selic rate at 14.00% by the end of 2026 for the fourth consecutive week. Financials traded higher, with Itaú, Bradesco, and Itaúsa adding more than 0.5%, while Banco do Brasil gained 0.5%. Most utility stocks also posted gains, with Axia and Sabesp edging higher. Steelmakers rebounded after coming under pressure last week following the US announcement of new tariffs on Brazilian imports, with Gerdau up more than 0.5% and CSN adding nearly 1.0%. Meanwhile, lower iron ore prices weighed on miners. Vale traded muted and CSN Mineração shed more than 0.5%.

【Goldman Sachs: Hedge funds selling US tech at record pace】Goldman Sachs’ prime brokerage said hedge funds have cut US technology positions at a record pace over the past two months. The Vincent Lin‑led team said funds were net sellers of the sector in six of the past eight weeks, with cumulative position value down about 10% — the largest reduction since the series began more than a decade ago. The team said sustained, large-scale selling since early June, amid sharp volatility and steep declines in semiconductors, memory chips and AI infrastructure, suggests material trimming of long exposure and early signs of capitulation.

US Stocks Start Week Higher — US stock indices rebounded from sharp losses on Monday after the heavyweight chip sector halted its selloff. The S&P 500 and the Nasdaq 100 gained more than 0.5%, while the Dow added 150 points. Heavyweight tech stocks were mostly higher as markets continued to gauge whether AI hyperscalers are due to slow spending on AI infrastructure in the next quarters, which have been the source of guidance upgrades for booming chip-manufacturing sector. Nvidia rose 1.5%, and Alphabet gained 2% ahead of its guidance update on Wednesday, the latest bellwether for both chip and software development capital expenditure. Meanwhile, fuel prices were elevated amid new attacks between Iran and the US and Houthi threats to attack Saudi tankers on the Red Sea. Still, yields held their pullback and remained below their recent peaks. Credit-sensitive bank holding companies remained higher from last week following strong earnings from the sector. Goldman Sachs and JPMorgan added 1% each.

【US equity short positions hit record highs as AI-driven rally raises concern】Short positions in US equities have climbed to record levels amid doubts about the sustainability of an AI-driven rally. S&P 500 constituents' short interest as a share of free float rose to 3.79%, a record in S3 Partners' data series dating to 2010, while Russell 3000 constituents' ratio climbed to 6.3%, also a record. The moves follow an S&P 500 gain of about 18% since end-March. Shorting has generally been an unsuccessful trade this year, but shorting activity and the universe of shorted stocks have expanded, S3 Partners LLC head of predictive analytics Ihor Dusaniwsky said.

【王曉暉主持召開四川省委財經委員會第十三次會議強調：始終保持高質量發展戰略定力，奮力推動經濟實現質的有效提升和量的合理增長】7月20日，四川省委財經委員會第十三次會議召開，省委書記、省委財經委員會主任王曉暉主持會議並講話。他強調，要深入貫徹習近平總書記對四川工作系列重要指示精神和黨中央決策部署，始終保持高質量發展戰略定力，錨定目標全力以赴補齊短板弱項，奮力推動經濟實現質的有效提升和量的合理增長。會議指出，要堅決穩住投資底線，全力扭轉負增長態勢。要堅持投資於物和投資於人協同發力，積極向上爭取“兩重”“兩新”等專案支援和各類資金支援，做好專案全生命週期服務管理，加力推進“六張網”規劃建設各項工作，推動投資止跌回升。要重視發揮投資基金的作用，進一步完善全省投資基金政策體系、運作模式和考評機制，堅持投早、投小、投長期、投硬科技，培育形成多元化、高質效的耐心資本生態。要發揮政府投資引導基金“四兩撥千斤”作用，吸引更多龍頭資本、創投機構參與我省重大戰略科技力量建設和重點產業培育發展；進一步增強國資國企基金的專業化運營效能，確保規範執行、風險可控；深化投融資體制改革，規範實施政府和社會資本合作新機制，不斷擴寬市場化投資空間、激發社會投資活力。（四川釋出）

### 2026-07-20 21:27:11  #市場 #國際

【歐盟擬暫緩實施甲烷排放違規處罰 以平衡能源安全與氣候目標】歐盟委員會正式透過一項非約束性指導建議，建議各成員國在2027年至2029年期間，暫不對違反歐盟甲烷排放法規的能源進口商實施處罰。此舉旨在緩解因能源供應安全引發的爭議，尋求氣候目標與能源穩定之間的平衡。根據歐盟現行甲烷監管框架，自2027年1月起，進口至歐盟的天然氣等化石燃料必須符合嚴格的監測、報告與核查（MRV）標準，並需在2030年前滿足具體的甲烷排放強度限制要求。不合規的進口商將面臨高額罰款。然而，由於全球能源供應鏈的複雜性，該法規引發了廣泛擔憂。美國、卡達等主要天然氣出口國近期多次向歐盟發出警告，指出在複雜的天然氣供應鏈中全程追蹤甲烷排放面臨極大的技術與物理障礙。出口國強調，由於法律合規是最高原則，若歐盟堅持推行該法規，進出口商將拒絕簽署明知可能違規的長期供應合同，這不僅將導致歐洲面臨天然氣短缺和價格上漲的風險，還可能迫使美國等國的液化天然氣（LNG）轉向其他市場。

【新能源汽車維修安全國標將實施】國家市場監督管理總局7月20日通報，《新能源汽車維修作業安全要求》（GB/T 47439—2026）國家標準將於8月1日起實施。該標準主要規定了新能源汽車維修的人員與場地、作業準備、風險排查與處置、作業流程和操作安全等要求。該標準聚焦新能源汽車高壓系統和氫燃料汽車供氫系統維修，提出了作業前、作業中和作業後的關鍵操作要求，重點明確了防護裝備及工具檢查、場地環境確認、作業風險識別以及B級電壓電路維修等內容。該標準的實施將有效指導新能源汽車維修人員規範作業和維修企業能力建設，進一步提升新能源汽車維修企業的安全風險防範能力和維修服務規範化水平，對保障新能源汽車維修安全和質量、促進維修行業安全發展具有重要作用。（新華社）

【谷歌研發全新“Frozen”專用晶片，大幅提升AI模型執行效率】據兩名知情人士透露，谷歌正在研發一款全新伺服器晶片，可將Gemini大模型的底層架構直接固化進晶片硬體，以此大幅提升面向使用者提供AI服務的執行效率。 這款內部代號Frozen v2的晶片，旨在解決谷歌當前AI算力嚴重短缺的難題。算力缺口已引發內部資源矛盾，甚至迫使谷歌雲拒絕了不少外部客戶的合作訂單。知情人士稱，研發團隊測算，該晶片正式落地後，單位功耗可處理的詞元（Token）數量，將達到谷歌現有自研AI晶片最新一代產品的6至10倍，能效實現質的飛躍。 “Frozen”這一代號的由來，源於其核心設計思路：將大模型的部分運算邏輯永久蝕刻在矽片硬體中。工程師仍在敲定這款晶片的核心功能模組，以及各元件協同工作的方案。知情人士表示，谷歌計劃最早2028年部署該晶片。（環球市場播報）

### 2026-07-20 21:00:56  #其他

【元腦伺服器推出CXL記憶體跨代擴充套件方案 實現DDR5和DDR4同機部署記憶體擴容】“元腦伺服器”公眾號訊息，元腦伺服器推出CXL（Compute Express Link）記憶體跨代擴充套件方案，實現了DDR5和DDR4同機部署記憶體擴容。基於元腦伺服器NF5280，在24條本地DDR5記憶體基本配置下，透過創新自研CXL記憶體擴充套件卡，單機最多可擴充套件16條DDR4記憶體，按照單條記憶體32GB容量，額外擴充套件512GB記憶體，整機記憶體容量提升33%，成本大幅降低25%。方案結合元腦伺服器作業系統KOS池化記憶體技術，將本地DRAM記憶體與CXL擴充套件記憶體融合為統一邏輯記憶體空間，透過冷熱資料識別、動態遷移和NUMA感知排程策略，讓擴充套件記憶體達到本地記憶體相當效能，提升CPU利用率30%。目前，該方案已在國內某大型網際網路客戶完成驗證並實現規模應用。

TSX Futures Gain on Softer Canadian Inflation — Futures tracking the S&P/TSX Composite Index edged higher on Monday following the release of June's inflation report. Canada's annual inflation rate eased to 2.8% in June 2026 from 3.2% in May, slightly below forecasts of 2.9%. Gasoline prices increased at a slower pace, while the Bank of Canada's preferred core inflation measures fell to their lowest levels in more than five years, reinforcing the BoC's view that the impact of higher energy costs stemming from the Middle East oil supply crisis is not spreading broadly throughout the economy. Canadian bond yields moved lower following the data release, supporting financials and the broader index. Meanwhile, oil prices remained elevated amid escalating tensions between the US and Iran and slow traffic through the Strait of Hormuz, while gold prices edged lower.

【Canada June CPI 2.8%; key core gauge falls to 1.85%】Statistics Canada on Monday said June CPI rose 2.8% YoY, down from 3.2% in May and below economists' 2.9% forecast. A key trimmed core inflation gauge fell to 1.85%, the lowest since September 2020 and the first reading under 2% in nearly six years. The decline was driven by lower gasoline prices, and Statistics Canada said inflationary effects from the Iran war have so far remained confined to energy. The Bank of Canada has warned that pass-through from higher energy could necessitate rate hikes, but the cooling in core inflation suggests economic slack is offsetting war-related price pressures.

### 2026-07-20 20:35:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Inflation Rate #Importance 3

Canada Inflation Slows More than Expected — The headline inflation rate in Canada fell to 2.8% in June of 2026 from the over-two-year high of 3.2% in the previous month, slightly under market expectations of 2.9%. The drop was consistent with a slower surge in prices of gasoline (20.5% vs 33.2% in May), trimming inflation for overall transportation (6.7% vs 9.0%), as the momentary restoration of energy exports from the Middle East had eased wholesale fuel prices. Price growth also eased for food (3.5% vs 3.8%) amid softer inflation in grocery stores. Meanwhile, the median core (1.9% vs 2.1%) and the trimmed-mean (1.8% vs 2%), both key core rates tracked by the BoC, fell more than expected to their lowest in over five years. Inflation fell for shelter (1.5% vs 1.7%) and health and personal care (2.5% vs 2.7%), while prices dropped for household operations, furnishings, and equipment (-0.2% vs -0.2%). From the previous month, the Canadian CPI fell by 0.4% amid a 10.2% drop in gasoline prices.

### 2026-07-20 20:16:18  #其他

【M87黑洞視界尺度輻射特性研究獲新進展】記者從中國科學院上海天文臺獲悉，近期，該天文臺科研團隊與國外合作者利用2018年事件視界望遠鏡（EHT）和全球毫米波VLBI（甚長基線干涉測量）陣列獲得的觀測資料，對M87黑洞開展了雙頻聯合光譜研究。研究利用1.3毫米和3.5毫米兩個波段獲得黑洞視界尺度影象，透過聯合分析，首次獲得了黑洞事件視界尺度的空間分辨譜指數分佈，並揭示了譜指數隨距離黑洞中心變化的規律。在這項研究中，譜指數反映了黑洞周圍不同位置的輻射在不同頻率下的亮度變化規律。這相當於為黑洞開展了一次高精度的“空間光譜體檢”。這也是迄今為止人類獲得的首張空間分辨的黑洞事件視界尺度譜指數影象。相關成果7月20日在國際學術期刊《天體物理學快報》發表。（央視新聞）

【東芯股份：預計2026年上半年淨利潤6.4億元至6.8億元】東芯股份公告，預計2026年上半年淨利潤6.4億元至6.8億元，與上年同期相比，將增加7.51億元至7.91億元，同比扭虧為盈。報告期內，受益於AI算力需求持續爆發及國內利基型儲存技術競爭力的增強，海外儲存原廠加速退出利基型市場，供給端收縮態勢明顯；與此同時，網路通訊、工業控制、汽車電子等下游應用需求穩步復甦，利基型儲存晶片供需缺口持續擴大。公司所面對的儲存晶片市場的供需關係呈現結構性緊缺態勢，產品市場價格大幅攀升。公司緊抓市場機遇，以SLC NAND Flash為代表的儲存業務實現量價齊升，帶動上半年營業收入實現大幅增長，整體毛利率亦顯著上漲，帶動上半年利潤水平的顯著提高。

【多款西班牙球衣售罄，阿迪達斯預計相關銷售超17億美元】北京時間7月20日，美加墨世界盃決賽吹響終場哨音。美國市場，曾被稱為足球的“荒漠”，然而，在本屆世界盃的成功舉辦之下，美國市場整體足球消費熱情顯著提升。與此同時，因西班牙奪冠而引發的相關球衣、周邊銷售被明顯拉動，整體世界盃經濟熱度升溫。研究機構Placer.ai資料顯示，世界盃首周，阿迪達斯美國門店到訪量較2026年日均水平飆升47%。阿迪達斯全球CEO比約恩·古爾登近日在美國接受當地媒體採訪時也表示，阿迪達斯本屆世界盃的球衣銷量是上屆卡達世界盃的四倍，足球銷量是上屆的兩倍。該公司預計與賽事相關的銷售額將超過17億美元。儘管勢頭正盛，但需要指出的是阿迪達斯仍有不小的差距要追趕。上一財年，其在北美市場的銷售額為55億美元，而耐克達到了205億美元。（第一財經）

### 2026-07-20 20:03:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Construction Output #Importance 2

India Infrastructure Output Picks Up — Infrastructure output in India jumped by 5.0% from the previous year in June of 2026 from the upwardly revised 3.2% increase in the previous month to mark the sharpest pace of growth since January. The result reflected some traction from the more fragile growth rates in the second quarter after the war in Iran triggered a surge in energy prices and costs that Indian manufacturers face. Output was high for construction inputs in iron ore (43.9% vs 19.0% in May), cement (9.8% vs 8.4%), and steel (4.6% vs 5.1%). Production also expanded for electricity (9.8% vs 11.2%). In turn, the supply shock from Persian Gulf energy exports reduced output for refinery products (-4.7% vs -8.2%).

【Burnham forms government, pledges reindustrialisation; Ukraine support unchanged】UK Prime Minister Burnham formally accepted the mandate to form a government and pledged to drive UK reindustrialisation via public procurement and returning key essentials to public control. He said powers will be devolved from Westminster to the regions and a new 10-year industrial plan will be published later this year. Cost-of-living measures — with a published cost assessment — will be announced tomorrow. Plans also include increased council housing and measures aimed at ending rough sleeping. Burnham said the government will continue to follow fiscal rules and meet defence commitments. His first foreign calls will be to US President Trump and Ukrainian President Zelenskiy; he said support for Ukraine will remain unchanged.

### 2026-07-20 20:02:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Rebounds as Markets Monitor Middle East Tensions — Silver rose to around $57 an ounce on Monday, recovering from an eight-month low as investors assessed the US-Iran conflict and its implications for inflation and interest rates. US forces continued striking Iranian targets, while concerns over shipping through the Strait of Hormuz intensified after Tehran reported that two oil tankers had exploded and been immobilized. Although oil prices eased after Iran signaled it remained open to negotiations with the US, Brent crude stayed near a one-month high, keeping inflation concerns elevated and reinforcing expectations that US interest rates will remain higher for longer. Meanwhile, Cleveland Fed President Beth Hammack joined a growing chorus of policymakers suggesting further rate hikes may be needed to curb persistent inflation. Markets are now pricing an 80% chance of a December Fed rate hike, up from 73% a week ago.

### 2026-07-20 19:59:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Gold Holds Steady as Markets Assess US-Iran Tensions — Gold hovered around $4,030 an ounce on Monday as investors assessed the evolving US-Iran conflict and its implications for oil prices, inflation, and monetary policy. US forces continued striking Iranian targets amid concerns over shipping through the Strait of Hormuz after Tehran reported that two oil tankers had exploded and been immobilized. Oil prices, which had climbed to a more than one-month high earlier in the session, later eased after Iran's foreign ministry said negotiations with the US could still be pursued if they aligned with the country's national interests. Even so, elevated energy prices continue to fuel inflation concerns and reinforce expectations that US interest rates will remain higher for longer. Meanwhile, Cleveland Fed President Beth Hammack joined a growing chorus of policymakers suggesting further rate hikes may be needed to contain persistent inflation. Markets are now pricing an 80% probability of a December Fed rate hike, up from 73% a week ago.

【韓國電商巨頭物流中心大火連燒3天 面臨垮塌風險】韓國電商巨頭酷澎公司一處物流中心18日燃起大火，火情持續3天仍未被完全控制。當地消防部門20日警告，如果火勢蔓延至存有大量鋰電池的區域，整個樓體將面臨垮塌風險。為防止險情發生，政府部門已組織附近居民疏散，並要求學校停課。這一物流中心位於首爾以西的仁川市，18日6時54分起火。根據當天釋出的國家消防動員令，當地已部署813名消防員和231件裝置參與滅火。消防部門官員20日說，火情雖得到一定控制，但將火完全撲滅所需時間“比預想要長”，加上該物流中心規模較大，目前無法預計完全控制火情的具體時間。（新華社）

### 2026-07-20 19:44:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Bund Yields Hold Near Two-Month High Ahead of ECB — Germany's 10-year Bund yield erased early gains to trade just above 3.1% as crude prices retreated on signs of renewed diplomatic efforts between the US and Iran. Despite the pullback, the benchmark yield remained close to a two-month high, while the more policy-sensitive two-year Bund yield climbed above 2.8%, its highest level since July 2024. Brent crude touched its highest level in more than a month as escalating US-Iran hostilities threatened oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz, fueling inflation concerns and reinforcing expectations that the European Central Bank will deliver two more rate hikes by early 2027, with the first increase expected as soon as September. Attention now turns to Thursday's ECB policy meeting, where policymakers are widely expected to leave rates unchanged and adopt a wait-and-see stance following June's first rate hike in three years.

【美國汽油均價重回每加侖4美元高位】美國汽車協會發布的資料顯示，全美普通汽油平均價格20日再次上漲至每加侖4美元以上水平。這一價格去年同期為每加侖3.14美元。此前，受美國和伊朗軍事衝突等因素影響，全美普通汽油平均價格今年3月底突破4美元，創近4年來新高，並持續上揚；美伊6月中旬簽署諒解備忘錄後，全美普通汽油平均價格回落至4美元以下並持續走低一段時間。近期，美伊持續互襲，油價再度攀升。國際原油期貨19日在開始新一週交易後價格顯著上漲，布倫特原油期貨價格突破每桶90美元。業界預計，霍爾木茲海峽阻塞時間越長，對全球經濟的衝擊越大，油價或將繼續攀升。美國將於11月迎來國會中期選舉，高油價已令美國總統特朗普和共和黨承壓。美國媒體16日釋出的民調結果顯示，在伊朗問題上，特朗普的不支援率升至69%；約67%的調查物件預計未來一年美國經濟會進一步惡化；約60%的人質疑美伊談判能否將汽油價格降至衝突前水平。（新華社）

### 2026-07-20 19:43:00  #宏觀 #焦點

【李強主持召開國務院常務會議】國務院總理李強7月20日主持召開國務院常務會議，聽取對服務業擴能提質和水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通訊網、城市地下管網、物流網等“六張網”規劃建設督查情況彙報，審議透過《智慧財產權保護和運用“十五五”規劃》，研究最佳化社會保障體系有關工作，聽取2025年度中央預算執行和其他財政收支審計查出問題整改工作建議彙報。

會議指出，推動服務業擴能提質和“六張網”規劃建設對於穩投資、擴消費、惠民生具有重要意義。要充分釋放服務業發展潛力，分門別類完善支援政策，加快破除各種制約和障礙。生產性服務業要加力支援科技創新和價值創造，進一步發揮對實體經濟的支撐作用，生活性服務業要著力擴大優質供給，更好滿足人民群眾多樣化多層次需求。要系統推進“六張網”規劃建設，因地因業加快實施，加強資金要素保障，切實把重大基礎設施網路建設好。同時統籌好建和用的關係，更加重視管理機制等“軟建設”，增強運營服務能力。

會議指出，近年來我國智慧財產權事業取得長足發展，為激發全社會創新活力、培育新質生產力提供有力支撐。要抓緊補齊制度短板，加快推動相關法律法規立改廢釋，完善配套政策措施，持續最佳化支援全面創新的智慧財產權保護環境。要強化智慧財產權高質量供給，深化權益分配改革，加強政策支援和市場服務，提升轉化運用效能。要建立全鏈條人才培養體系，加快建設高層次、複合型、應用型人才隊伍，全面提升智慧財產權管理服務水平。

會議指出，要提高社會保障體系的整體性協同性，最佳化相關制度安排，加強銜接聯動，高效配置各種資源，增強社保制度可持續發展能力。要深化政策機制創新，豐富多層次社保體系，積極探索靈活就業人員社保新模式，最佳化農民工等群體住房保障措施，推進失能照護等服務資源共享，增強社保體系適應性。要完善社保平臺功能，加強精細化管理，積極運用先進技術提高社保經辦服務便利度。

會議指出，要全面抓好審計整改工作，對審計查出問題高度重視、嚴肅對待，逐項分解任務、壓緊壓實責任，舉一反三務求整改實效。要進一步強化預算管理，深化財稅體制改革，切實發揮好財政資金的使用效益。要把審計整改作為改進工作的有力抓手，推動黨中央決策部署和政策措施更好落地見效，紮實做好下半年各項工作，確保完成全年經濟社會發展目標任務。（新華社）

Gilt Yields Ease as Markets Weigh Oil and Burnham’s Government — The UK 10-year gilt yield eased to 4.95%, although it remained close to a two-month high, as crude prices retreated on signs of renewed diplomatic efforts between the US and Iran. 【中國與泰國簽署加強產供鏈經濟合作的諒解備忘錄】7月20日，在國務院總理李強和泰國總理阿努廷共同見證下，中國商務部部長王文濤與泰國副總理兼商業部長素帕姬在北京簽署了《中華人民共和國商務部和泰王國商業部關於加強產供鏈經濟合作的諒解備忘錄》(以下簡稱《備忘錄》)。根據《備忘錄》，雙方同意推進兩國產供鏈經濟合作，定期舉行會議、加強交流，支援兩國相關政府部門、地方、企業等不斷深化和促進務實合作，更好為兩國企業營造良好營商環境，共同應對挑戰。近年來，中泰產供鏈合作潛力持續釋放。《備忘錄》的簽署，將推動中泰建立務實高效的產供鏈合作平臺，維護兩國產供鏈安全、韌性和暢通，共同打造更為緊密的中泰產供鏈經濟合作關係。（商務部）

### 2026-07-20 19:33:19  #宏觀 #焦點

【商務部與柬埔寨商業部簽署《關於在第三國貿易措施背景下加強合作與相互理解的諒解備忘錄》】近日，中國商務部部長王文濤與柬埔寨商業部大臣佔尼莫簽署《關於在第三國貿易措施背景下加強合作與相互理解的諒解備忘錄》。

雙方同意，將繼續就與第三國貿易措施相關的共同關心問題，保持開放、建設性的對話。在可能影響雙邊經貿關係的有關措施解讀和實施上，雙方會加強溝通、增進理解、提高透明度。

柬方申明，其與第三方的貿易安排不會影響與中國簽署的任何協議、更不會損害中國利益。柬方沒有、也不會採取或實施任何針對或實質性針對中國並妨礙中國與東盟經貿合作的歧視性措施。

備忘錄的簽署，體現了中柬源遠流長的友誼、全面戰略合作伙伴關係以及深厚持久的政治、經濟和人文聯絡。這有助於雙方進一步加強合作與相互理解，對雙方在尊重主權、相互信任和合作共贏基礎上構建新時代全天候中柬命運共同體具有重要意義。（商務部）

【China MIIT to deepen "AI+Manufacturing" drive, boost computing power, models and data supply】China's Ministry of Industry and Information Technology (MIIT) said it will deepen the "AI+Manufacturing" initiative, focusing on application-led deployment in manufacturing. The ministry will cultivate AI application service providers and implement a "model–data resonance" action to roll out lightweight, low-cost, easy-to-deploy industry solutions that lower SME adoption barriers. MIIT will strengthen supply of core technologies — computing power, AI models and data — accelerate R&D of intelligent equipment and next‑generation smart manufacturing, and support iteration of consumer smart terminals as well as wider deployment of humanoid robots and brain‑computer interfaces. It will also expand a national AI open‑source community to share high‑quality models and datasets, step up development and promotion of key standards, and reinforce industry security safeguards.

### 2026-07-20 19:04:05  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indian Stocks Close Lower — Indian stocks closed sharply lower on Monday, tracking the losses from Wall Street on Friday and pressured by more macroeconomic risks to India. The S&P/BSE Sensex fell 0.6% to 77,709 and the NSE Nifty 50 dropped 0.4% to 24,239. Banks led the losses in the session, pressured by higher credit costs and more capital outflows from India due to fresh pressure on the rupee. Another session of increases in oil prices lifted expected capital outflows as energy importers transact in dollars, limiting foreign exposure to Indian stocks. The Kotak Mahindra Bank fell 2.1%, while Axis Bank and HDFC Bank lost more than 5% after both holding companies reported their earnings for the June quarter. Meanwhile, a fresh selloff for tech companies in the US was felt in Mumbai due to the large exposure of North American clients for Indian companies. TCS and Infosys both closed 0.6% lower.

【China coast guard says Philippine grounded vessel No.57 deliberately provoked near Ren'ai Reef】China coast guard said on July 20 at about 09:00 a.m. one of its small patrol vessels on routine patrol near Ren'ai Reef in the South China Sea was approached by two rubber boats launched from the Philippine illegally grounded No.57 vessel. The boats ignored repeated Chinese warnings, approached at speed in a dangerous manner and collided with the Chinese patrol boat; Philippine personnel allegedly attacked Chinese law-enforcement officers with paddles and poles. China said it responded with verbal warnings, maneuvering and proportional countermeasures, exercising maximum restraint. Beijing accused the Philippines of distorting the incident, demanded an immediate halt to what it called incursions and false reporting, and warned the China coast guard will continue law-enforcement operations around the Spratly Islands, including Ren'ai Reef, to defend territorial sovereignty and maritime rights.

【多家銀行“鬆綁”高階信用卡】高階信用卡成為本輪最佳化的重點。從下調附屬卡年費、簡化年費減免規則，到降低消費達標門檻、推出階段性優惠活動，銀行在年費政策上的動作明顯增多。與過去兩年行業持續壓縮高階信用卡權益、提高年費減免門檻不同，此輪調整更多體現為結構性最佳化，並非簡單降低收費標準，透過最佳化年費規則提升客戶體驗、增強客戶黏性，反映出信用卡市場競爭重心正由新增獲客轉向存量經營，行業競爭逐步進入“精耕細作”階段。（介面）

---

### 2026-07-20 18:55:59  #宏觀 #焦點

【國家發展改革委主任鄭柵潔主持召開民營企業座談會，圍繞上半年經濟形勢和做好下半年經濟工作聽取意見建議】7月20日，國家發展改革委主任鄭柵潔主持召開民營企業座談會，與來自食品加工、工業自動化測試裝置製造、營銷服務、人形機器人研發、物聯網服務等領域的三全食品、華興源創、易點天下、銀河通用機器人、飛尚科技等5家企業負責人溝通交流，圍繞上半年經濟形勢和下半年經濟工作聽取意見建議。會上，參會企業負責人圍繞會議主題，結合各自行業特點與企業生產經營情況等，對上半年經濟形勢進行了分析。大家普遍認為，今年以來外部不穩定不確定因素增多，我國經濟頂住壓力、穩中有進，展現出強大韌性和活力，目前企業生產經營平穩，市場預期穩步改善，有能力、有信心完成全年生產經營目標。參會企業還圍繞推動產業鏈上下游協同、促進傳統產業改造升級、加大創新人才培養力度、加強資料等要素保障、規範招標投標市場秩序等方面提出意見建議。鄭柵潔認真傾聽參會企業負責人發言，並與大家互動交流，逐一回應訴求建議。他表示，企業對經濟執行的感知和判斷，為我們監測分析經濟形勢、研究制定政策舉措、實施宏觀調控提供了積極參考，企業有活力，經濟才有動力。今年是“十五五”開局之年，在以習近平同志為核心的黨中央堅強領導下，宏觀政策持續發力，上半年我國經濟總體平穩，新質生產力培育壯大，高質量發展向新向優。雖然外部環境變化帶來不少困難挑戰，但我國經濟長期向好的支撐條件和基本趨勢沒有改變，希望廣大民營企業家堅定發展信心，深入挖掘需求升級和產業升級機遇，堅守主業、做強實業，為推動經濟社會高質量發展多作貢獻。做好下半年經濟工作對完成全年發展目標十分重要。國家發展改革委將深入貫徹落實黨中央和國務院決策部署，發揮好存量政策和增量政策整合效應，加快培育消費新增長點，持續推動“十五五”規劃綱要重大工程專案實施，推進新舊動能平穩接續轉換，加快場景培育和開放，進一步構建高質量資料供給體系，縱深推進全國統一大市場建設，不斷增強經濟發展內生動力，激發市場活力。同時，實施好“法治護航民營經濟”行動，加力推動民營經濟促進法配套制度建設，發揮好多層次與民營企業常態化溝通交流機制作用，持續營造良好環境，全力促進民營經濟高質量發展。國家發展改革委副主任沈竹林，辦公廳、綜合司、民營局主要負責同志參會。

---

### 2026-07-20 18:45:24  #宏觀 #焦點 #國際

【李強同泰國總理阿努廷會談】7月20日上午，國務院總理李強在北京人民大會堂同來華進行正式訪問的泰國總理阿努廷舉行會談。

李強表示，中泰是有著上千年友好交往的親密鄰邦。當前，在兩國領導人戰略引領下，中泰命運共同體建設正不斷走深走實，為雙方合作開闢更加廣闊前景。總理先生這次訪華期間，習近平主席同你舉行會晤，就深化發展兩國關係作出新的規劃。中方始終把泰國置於周邊外交重要位置，堅定支援泰國新一屆政府順利施政，願同泰方進一步弘揚傳統友誼，鞏固政治互信，堅定相互支援，推動各領域互利合作邁向更高水平，持續提升兩國人民福祉。

李強指出，中方願同泰方加強發展戰略對接，推動高質量共建“一帶一路”，加快實施中泰鐵路等重點專案，提升跨境支付、本幣結算等便利化水平，保持雙邊貿易良好增長勢頭，深化產業互融互嵌，拓展人工智慧、新能源汽車、清潔能源等新興領域合作，攜手促進共同繁榮。希望泰方為赴泰中國企業提供良好營商環境。雙方還要深化旅遊、教育、媒體等人文交流，厚植民間友好基礎，同時進一步加強執法安全合作，堅決打擊網賭電詐等跨境犯罪。當前，單邊主義、保護主義愈演愈烈。中泰同為開放合作的踐行者、受益者，應當緊密團結協作，在聯合國、世貿組織、中國－東盟、亞太經合組織、瀾湄合作等多邊機制加強協調配合，共同維護兩國正當權益和國際公平正義。

阿努廷表示，熱烈祝賀中國成功舉辦2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議。泰中一家親，哇集拉隆功國王去年訪華，同習近平主席舉行成功會晤，為泰中關係開啟下一個金色50年注入新動力。泰方堅定奉行一箇中國政策，永遠不會支援任何“臺獨”行徑。泰方願同中方密切高層往來，用好各領域對話機制，促進貿易、投資、農業、互聯互通、人工智慧、電動汽車等領域合作，深化旅遊、地方交流，推動泰中全面戰略合作伙伴關係取得更多務實成果。泰方將加大打擊網賭電詐等跨境犯罪力度，為赴泰中國遊客和企業營造良好、安全環境。泰方歡迎習近平主席提出的四大全球倡議，願同中方在東盟－中國、瀾湄合作等多邊框架內加強溝通協調，共促地區與世界和平、穩定、繁榮。

會談後，兩國總理共同見證簽署貿易、農業、海關、科技、人工智慧教育、航空航天、智慧財產權、媒體、打擊跨國犯罪等領域多項合作檔案。（新華社）

【Hua Chuang Securities chief economist Zhang Yu: AI support, full industrial chain and ample policy room underpin markets】Hua Chuang Securities chief economist Zhang Yu said the recent short-term A‑share pullback was triggered by external factors: geopolitics pushed oil higher, lifting US inf and tightening rate expectations, and a synchronous overseas equity sell-off transmitted sentiment and funding shocks that amplified domestic volatility. On fundamentals, midstream manufacturing activity in China remains robust and the global capital‑goods supply‑demand backdrop favors domestic producers, leaving ample sector growth space. Over the long term, AI support policies, a complete industrial ecosystem and ample policy room and fiscal reserves provide multiple supports for markets. Zhang said the decline primarily reflects valuation and sentiment rebalancing, not a reversal of domestic fundamentals or China’s manufacturing competitiveness; external shocks may change market timing but are unlikely to derail the long‑term tech and industrial upgrade trend.

【18只高股息股7月以來獲得融資淨買入】據證券時報·資料寶統計，以2025年度的每股派現金額及最新收盤價為基準，對上市公司股息率進行統計發現，共有197家股息率超過5%，其中，榮晟環保股息率最高，超過15%；股息率較高的還有匯潔股份、冰川網路等，股息率均超10%。高股息個股7月以來普遍上漲，平均漲幅4.03%，53股累計漲幅在10%以上，超過總數的四分之一。立霸股份、鄂爾多斯、濟川藥業3股累計漲幅居前，均超過20%。值得注意的是，儘管經歷一輪上漲，仍有相當比例的高股息股估值處於相對低位。統計顯示，44股最新市淨率不足1倍，仍處於“破淨”狀態。從行業分佈看，銀行股是“破淨”高股息的主力陣營，共有13只入圍，此外，建築裝飾、交通運輸、公用事業等行業亦有多隻個股在列。銀行板塊上半年曆經深度回撥，1~6月累計下跌12.27%，進入7月，板塊反彈勢頭強勁，年內整體跌幅已顯著收窄至4.52%。目前，銀行行業整體市淨率僅為0.5倍，處於2025年以來17.2%的較低歷史分位，修復空間依然充裕。資金面上來看，18只高股息股7月以來獲得融資淨買入，中聯重科居於首位，融資淨買入5.24億元，吉位元、申能股份、義翹神州緊隨其後，分別為3595.22萬元、1657.6萬元、1259.89萬元。（證券時報）

### 2026-07-20 18:18:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Youth Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

China Youth Unemployment Rate Lowest in a Year — China's urban youth unemployment rate for people aged 16–24, excluding students, fell for a third consecutive month to 14.9% in June 2026, the lowest level in a year, from 15.6% in May, according to the National Bureau of Statistics. The decline points to a continued improvement in youth employment amid government efforts to stabilize the labor market, support economic growth, and better align university programs with labor market needs. Even so, the rate remained above the 14.5% recorded in June 2025. Meanwhile, the unemployment rate for 25–29-year-olds, excluding students, edged down to 7.1% from 7.2%, while the jobless rate for the 30–59 age group eased to 4.0% from 4.1%. The overall surveyed urban unemployment rate also declined slightly, to 5.0% from 5.1%.

### 2026-07-20 18:15:16  #市場 #觀點 #央行 #焦點 #國際

【資金“空轉多”！創業板50ETF華安（159949）成交45.40億領跑同類 機構：靜待修復性反彈行情】7月20日，市場全天震盪整理，早間衝高回落，午後快速下跌，臨近尾盤，科創50指數、創業板指深V拉昇翻紅。受此帶動，創業板50ETF華安（159949）上漲0.73%，報1.651元，換手率21.52%，成交額45.40億元，居同類標的ETF首位。訊息面上，工業和資訊化部新聞發言人謝存表示，新一代通訊網不僅是傳統意義上的網路升級，更是面向智慧時代數字資訊基礎設施的一次關鍵革新。下一步，工信部將把新一代通訊網規劃建設作為推動資訊通訊業發展的重點。資金面上，創業板50ETF華安（159949）由淨流出轉為淨流入。近60個交易日資金淨流出4771萬，而近5個交易日資金淨流入18.96億元，近10個交易日資金淨流入20.52億元。截止2026年7月17日，該ETF流通規模208.24億。中信證券認為，新一輪的行情正在醞釀過程中，靜待各類敘事的扭轉以開啟中期行情空間，目前密切關注北美雲廠商月底的指引、市場情緒和量價指標、AI通脹邏輯弱化後美聯儲的政策取向以及月底重要會議預期。華安證券指出，近期調整幅度明顯高於歷史可比情形，在於非理性恐慌，卻更強化對市場處於第二階段行情的信心，短期調整到位甚至超調後，無需再悲觀，修復性反彈預計很快來臨。配置上，AI算力硬體大幅降溫調整後，正是新入局良機。科技板塊中報預告延續超高景氣，產業趨勢行情遠未結束，需堅定信心。投資者可直接透過股票賬戶交易創業板50ETF華安（159949），也可透過聯接基金（A類：160422；C類：160424；I類：022654；Y類：022976）參與。

### 2026-07-20 18:10:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Portugal Producer Inflation Eases in June — Industrial producer inflation in Portugal eased to 5.0% year-on-year in June 2026, slowing from 5.1% in the previous month. The moderation was primarily driven by a slowdown in energy inflation, which eased to 12.7% from 19.5%, as producer prices in the utilities sector fell 1.7% after rising 7.5% in May. In contrast, price growth accelerated across the remaining major categories. Intermediate goods posted the strongest increase (6.3% vs. 4.6%), followed by non-durable consumer goods (1.1% vs. 0.6%), durable consumer goods (2.0% vs. 0.6%) and capital goods (2.2% vs. 1.8%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 0.8% in June, easing from a 1.0% increase in the previous month.

### 2026-07-20 18:07:27  #其他

【芬蘭研製出基於超導電路的迴圈量子熱機】芬蘭阿爾託大學研究人員新研製出一種基於超導電路的迴圈量子熱機，並透過實驗證明，在量子尺度上微弱的熱量也可以轉化為可測量的能量輸出。該研究成果已發表在英國《自然-通訊》雜誌上。 阿爾託大學日前釋出的新聞公報說，熱機是將熱能轉化為可利用能量的裝置，蒸汽機、汽車發動機和許多發電裝置都依據這一原理執行。近年來，隨著量子技術發展，科學家開始探索熱力學定律在量子尺度下如何發揮作用，以及能否利用量子系統中的微小熱量完成能量轉換。 此次研製的量子熱機僅有數毫米大小，由超導量子位元、諧振器和量子製冷裝置組成，工作原理是將冷卻到接近絕對零度的量子系統損失的少量熱能轉化為可用能量。實驗結果顯示，在接近絕對零度的環境中，量子系統中的微弱熱量能夠轉化為可測量的能量輸出。(新華社)

Chronert says this cluster represents over half of S&P 500 market capitalisation and contributed roughly 48% of earnings. After powering repeated S&P 500 highs in recent years, the 'Tech Seven' cohort lagged the market in 2026 as investors favoured stocks expected to gain from large AI capex.

### 2026-07-20 17:59:52  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bullish #SSE Composite #Hang Seng Index #Impact mixed

【China Merchants Securities: market showing differentiated bottoming — "growth purges bubbles, value stabilizes the base"】China Merchants Securities research center says the current correction is structural deleveraging after overcrowding in a single tech theme, not a systemic leverage crisis, and the mid‑term repair foundation is gradually consolidating. Market risk clearing is concentrated: no large‑scale off‑exchange high‑leverage financing; risks are focused on AI and semiconductors, while value sectors are providing strong support, producing a differentiated bottoming pattern described as "growth purges bubbles, value stabilizes the base." The cleanup is in its latter phase: on‑exchange margin financing balances have declined for 11 consecutive trading days and marginal selling pressure is easing; equity ETFs saw record weekly net inflows; previously strong tracks such as optical modules have experienced catch‑down falls, a pattern consistent with late‑stage panic liquidation, limiting systemic scope for further index declines.
